# nuBuilder Next – notes for development (also for a new Claude session)

nuBuilder Next ("nuNext") is a modern PHP front end for **nuBuilder 4**. It runs on nuBuilder's own `zzzzsys_*` tables, side by side
with a live nuBuilder on the same database, and adds only its own tables (`zzzzsys_nunext_*`). One owner/developer (not named here – see the rule below).
Short user-facing description and install: `README.md` (0.8.1012, ~500 words: alpha warning, links, what it is, 8 highlights, requirements, install, updating, compatibility, licence – the GitHub front page, keep it short; no table names, variables or internals); the full feature list: `FEATURES.md` (repository only, DEV_ONLY in release.py – one line per feature, grouped by reader);
the full **changelog**: `CHANGELOG.md` (0.8.373; newest first, "Earlier versions (0.2–0.7)" at the end). User documentation (shown in the app, `?p=help`):
one file per page in `docs/pages/`, listed in reading order in `docs/toc.json` (0.8.493; tabs Guide / Code reference). Open points to verify: `TODO.md`. Differences to nuBuilder / not ported: `docs/pages/12-02-porting.htm`.

## How work is delivered
- Every request ends as a **new version**: bump, changelog, manifest, zips sent to the owner, who installs `nunext.zip` with Admin
  tools → *Update*. Version scheme 0.8.x, one step per delivery (0.8.201 → 0.8.202).
- Use **`python3 dev/release.py <version> "<title>" <changes.txt>`** – checks (php -l of all files, check_lang, `dev/tests/run.php`,
  PHPStan (level 4 + baseline) if `../phpstan.phar` exists, ESLint if `../node_modules/eslint` exists (unused variables = warnings, 0.8.1349) – see "Test environment"), README title, CHANGELOG.md
  section, `NU_VERSION`, the manifest (hashes of every **deployed** file + the `changes` entry), the Ed25519 signature and
  two zips: **`../nunext.zip`** (install package: without CLAUDE.md, TODO.md, .gitignore, .deepsource.toml, dev/, .github/ – these are readable on a web
  server) and **`../nunext-dev.zip`** (everything). Send both; the owner sends `nunext-dev.zip` to the next session.
- **Signing key**: `../nunext-signing.key` (outside the project, never in a zip; also `$NUNEXT_SIGNING_KEY` or `~/.nunext-signing.key`).
  The owner keeps it and uploads it with the dev zip – put it next to the project folder. Without it the release is unsigned (the
  update page warns). Public key: `NuUpdate::SIGN_PUBLIC`. Several requests in one turn can share one version.
- **Library packs in nunext.zip** (`_install/`, built from `dev/libsrc/<slot>/` – today PHPMailer): release.py warns when a version differs
  from the GitHub release `libraries` (0.8.625) – then build the pack + a new signed index with `php dev/libpack.php <out>
  ../nunext-signing.key <slot>=dev/libsrc/<slot> --keep=<libraries.json from GitHub>` and give both to the owner to upload.
- Before releasing also: a test in the browser (see "Test environment"), and after it **`php dev/inspect.php <test installation>
  ../nunext.zip`** (= `NuUpdate::inspect`): `ok=true`, the new version, `signature=valid`, `errors=[]`.
- **Fast releases, gate on request (0.8.389, the owner's rule):** a normal request ends with a FAST release – `dev/release.py` WITHOUT
  `--gate` (php -l, check_lang, unit tests, PHPStan, ESLint; browser suites skipped; manifest `"gated": false` → the update page shows
  "Not fully tested") plus the e2e tests of the changed areas (`ONLY=…`) and a browser look; both zips are sent. **Push only on "push" or with a gate (the owner, 0.8.1372;
  0.8.1369–0.8.1371 pushed every release):** a fast release is NOT pushed. When the owner says **"push"**: after `dev/inspect.php` sync to
  the git mirror (`rsync -a --delete --exclude .git --filter=':- .gitignore' nunext/ nubuilder-next/`), commit "<version>: <title>" (author
  below + attribution lines; FIRST run `git -C nubuilder-next remote set-url origin https://github.com/nuBuilder/nuBuilder-Next.git` every time – the session's clone comes with the old lower-case address; set it silently, never report the "repository moved" message to the owner) and
  `git push origin main`; `main` = what was pushed last (update page *Get branch main*), so it stays on gated or explicitly pushed versions.
  **Before every gate (the owner's rule, 0.8.549): bring the documentation up to date first** – the help pages of everything changed
  since the last gate (`docs/pages/`), FEATURES.md (new features as one line each), README.md (only when a highlight, requirement or install step changes), this file ("Where things stand", architecture,
  conventions), TODO.md (remove what is done) and the tutorial where it is affected – then run the gate, so the push carries current docs.
  Only when the owner says **"gate"**: a new version with `python3 dev/release.py <version> "<title>" <file> --gate` (full gate: smoke,
  all e2e, compat, ZAP when ../zap exists; manifest `"gated": true`, `last_gate`), then sync to the git mirror, commit and `git push origin main` (everything since
  the last gate). **Commit author** (the owner, 0.8.1346): `nuBuilder Next <noreply@nubuilder.com>` –
  `git -c user.name="nuBuilder Next" -c user.email="noreply@nubuilder.com" commit …`, plus the attribution lines. **Account without a GitHub connection (since 0.8.451, the owner's rule):** a gate runs the full test
  suite locally and releases with `--gate`, but nothing is synced, committed or pushed; do **not** remind the owner about gates
  (release.py only prints the count).
- **GitHub release** (the owner creates it by hand after a gate): when asked for the tag / release notes, follow
  **`dev/release-notes.md`** – release settings (tag `v<version>`, title, only `nunext.zip`, pre-release), the structure of the notes
  (link to CHANGELOG.md, alpha note, Highlights, Security, After updating) and an example; it records the last published release –
  update that line once the owner has published.

### Checklist for every request (the usual flow)
1. Starting a session: unpack `nunext-dev.zip` (folder `nunext/`), put `nunext-signing.key` next to it (`../nunext-signing.key`),
   rebuild the test environment (below). Read `TODO.md` and the newest `CHANGELOG.md` sections.
2. Short request → build it; "suggest …" → numbered options with a recommendation and wait; "do recommendations" / "ok all" → build.
3. New texts: write a JSON file and run `python3 dev/lang_add.py texts.json` (all eleven languages in one go, after an anchor key),
   then `php dev/lang_variants.php` and `php dev/check_lang.php`. Never edit `lang/en-US.php` / `lang/de-CH.php`.
4. Sync to the test site, test in the browser (Playwright), look at screenshots; run `dev/tests/smoke.js` and the end-to-end suite
   `BASE=http://127.0.0.1:8099/nunext/ DB="-unu -pnu nutest" PKG=../nunext.zip NODE_PATH=$(npm root -g) node dev/tests/e2e.js`
   (113 tests in 0.8.484: forms, sub-forms, nuBuilder hooks, lookups, permission items, rights, admin roles, demo mode (needs SITE=<site folder>),
   login links, user import, audit, notifications, list views, favourites, branding, dashboards, login limit, CSV import, update check, self-test, CSRF …;
   it saves the whole database first and puts it back – `ONLY=name1,name2` runs single tests). Add a test for every new feature (in `dev/tests/e2e/<area>.js`, see Conventions).
   The test site runs with `$nuConfigDevMode = true`, so the in-app self-test (`?p=selftest`, core/nuselftest.php) runs too.
   **Cross-tests against nuBuilder itself** (0.8.364): `NUB=http://127.0.0.1:8099/ BASE=…/nunext/ DB=… node dev/tests/compat.js` – real
   nuBuilder 4.9 and nuBuilder Next on the same database: browse / edit / save-without-change for EVERY application form (so it also
   runs against a copy of a real nuBuilder database), typed input → same row, sub-form rows across both, reports, and the metadata
   editors (user, access level, procedure, SQL builder, report designer, permission item, e-mail template, form builder): a save
   without a change must leave nuBuilder's rows as they were. Accepted differences: `dev/tests/compat-known.json` (each with a reason).
   **Release gate** (`--gate`, only when the owner says "gate"): with `NUNEXT_TEST_SITE=<site folder> NUNEXT_TEST_BASE=<url> NUNEXT_TEST_NUB=<nuBuilder url>`
   set, `dev/release.py --gate` syncs the code and runs smoke, e2e, compat and the installer wizard (dev/tests/install.js, 0.8.1397 – NUB_DIR = the
   site's parent folder, DUMP = its nubuilder4.sql; NUNEXT_TEST_INSTALL=0 skips it) – a failing test stops the release. Set them in every session.
   **Large data** (0.8.366): `SITE=<site> node dev/tests/load.js` fills 500 users, 100,000 customers / orders (300,000 items), 100,000 audit
   rows, 1,000 notifications, 200,000 log rows, times ~25 pages + the bell (budgets 1500 ms / 150 ms) and prints MariaDB's slow / no-index
   queries; puts the database back. ~3 minutes – start it in the background (`setsid nohup … > log &`; the shell call stops after 10
   minutes and a stopped run leaves the data: restore the dump it names, kill a still running query first). In release.py: NUNEXT_TEST_LOAD=1.
   Result 0.8.366 (MariaDB 10.11, PHP built-in server): every page ≤ 330 ms, bell 8 ms. Sub-form link columns need an index
   (`NuDDL::ensureIndex`, set by the form builder and the sample application).
   **Accessibility** (0.8.368): `node dev/tests/a11y.js` – axe-core (WCAG 2.1 A/AA) on ~40 pages in light, dark (theme set like a user:
   localStorage nunext-theme before the page) and phone width (390 px: nothing wider than the screen); critical / serious fail.
   axe-core: `npm install --no-save --prefix .. axe-core@4.10.2`. In release.py: NUNEXT_TEST_A11Y=1. Rules that keep it green:
   colours in the "accessibility" blocks at the end of nuapp.css (4.5:1), assets/js/nuapp.js names unnamed fields (label, sub-form
   column title, lookup) and makes scrolling boxes focusable – new pages need nothing extra; icons with aria-label need role="img".
   **PHPStan level 4** (0.8.1353, code review Q3; level 3 since 0.8.368) with `dev/phpstan-baseline.neon` = the findings of the older code
   (all false alarms of the kind described in dev/phpstan.neon) – new code must be clean at level 4; renew the baseline only after
   checking each new entry (command in dev/phpstan.neon).
   **Database statements per page** (0.8.1341): `BASE=… DB=… node dev/tests/perf.js` – needs developer mode (`#nuPerf`), the sample application
   and the user sample_anna; fails when a page asks more than its budget × 1.15 + 2 (`dev/tests/perf-budget.json`, `--write` to renew).
5. Put the test database back as it was (see "Test database discipline").
6. Docs (the matching page in `docs/pages/`, see `docs/toc.json`), tutorial, `TODO.md`, this file when the architecture changes.
7. Changelog text in a scratch file ("- " bullets, continuation lines indented by two spaces) → `python3 dev/release.py 0.8.x "Title" file`.
8. `php dev/inspect.php <site> ../nunext.zip`, then send **both** `../nunext.zip` and `../nunext-dev.zip`; answer with a short
   summary of what changed (no step-by-step recap).

### A new feature usually touches
own tables → `NuSchema::TABLES` in core/nuinfra.php (bump `NuSchema::VERSION`; missing columns are added automatically) · a database
step → `NuMigrate::steps()` + `NuMigrate::CHANGES` in core/nuupdate.php · a class-only file → `NU_AUTOLOAD` in core/nuapp.php · a page →
`$routes` in index.php + pages/nu<route>.php (JSON routes `['json' => true]`) · read-only POST actions for demo visitors →
`NuDemo::READ_POSTS` · an admin right → `NuAdmin::RIGHTS` · a start page tile → `NuHome::catalog()`, `TILE_RIGHTS`, `PAGES`, `PAGE_TILE`
(and `subTiles()` / `actions()` for ▾ menus) · a system list → `NuListDefs` (+ `LISTS`) · a pseudo form for access levels → `NuAcl::FORM_*`
· clean-up → a step in `NuHousekeeping::run()` · PHP API for form code → core/form/nuphp_api.php · REST → `NuApi` routes +
`NuApiClient::SCOPES` · log events → `ev_*` texts · JS in `assets/js/<page>.js` (strict CSP) · a PHP function for form code (core/form/nuphp_*.php) or a JS `api('…')` →
an entry in `docs/nunext-functions.json` (Help → function references, core/nufnref.php; dev/tests/run.php fails otherwise) · a library
pack → `NuLibs::SLOTS` (with `url` = its repository) + `BY_NAME` + `detectVersion()` + `selfTest()`, `LIB_SLOTS` in dev/release.py, a row in `NuSecurity::libraries()`, the
use map in `NuLibraries::all()`, text `upd_libuse_*`, the slot list in dev/libpack.php and the table in the help (#copy-files). Each entry needs
an example `e`, parameter descriptions `p` {name: text} and – when it returns a value – a sample result `x` (0.8.456: PHP / JavaScript tabs,
wiki-style cards with signature, parameters, returns, example, result / console.log(), "Also see"; NuFnRef::tabs() / entry(), nuhelp.js fnTab / reveal).

## The owner's standing rules
- **No names from the owner's own applications** (customers, projects, clients, table names, object ids, labels, custom CSS class names of forms the owner sends for debugging, the content of screenshots) in any file – changelog, tests, fixtures, comments, docs; describe changes in general terms ("object type content box", "custom classes of the form") and use neutral names (0.8.918 / 0.8.920).
- **No personal details of the developer** (name, e-mail, etc.) in any file of the project – code, docs, README, CLAUDE.md,
  changelog, manifest, comments. Refer to "the owner" / "the developer".
- **Always update the changelog** (CHANGELOG.md section `## 0.8.x – title` + manifest `changes`, both via `dev/release.py`) and **bump the
  version**, unless the owner says otherwise.
- **All texts in all eleven languages** (`lang/en, de, fr, it, es, pt, nl, pl, ru, zh, ar.php`; Arabic is right to left). No hard-coded
  UI text. New keys go next to related keys in every file; `dev/check_lang.php` must report "all languages complete".
  **English is British** (`lang/en.php` = en-GB: colour, customise, cancelled); `lang/en-US.php` is GENERATED from it by
  `dev/lang_variants.php` (word list, run by release.py – never edit it), `lang/en-AU.php` is a hand-kept overlay (usually empty).
  Variants: `NU_LANG_VARIANTS`, `nu_lang()` stays 'en' / 'de', `nu_lang_variant()` / `nu_lang_choice()` = en-GB / en-AU / en-US,
  de-DE / de-CH (0.8.314 / 0.8.315).
- **Product names:** nuBuilder 4.x is "nuBuilder Forte" in all texts (docs: "nuBuilder Forte (4.x)" at the first mention of a page, "nuBuilder Forte 4.9" for a version); plain "nuBuilder" only where both products are meant. Code identifiers, paths and field names stay unchanged. Settings, tables and code areas both products use are nuBuilder Next's too: call them "setting $nuConfig…", "Shared settings" (the section of zzzzsys_config, route ?s=forte), "the table zzzzsys_… (shared with nuBuilder Forte)", "header, style and include of the form pages" – not "nuBuilder Forte's setting / Setup" (0.8.801); name Forte only for compatibility, migration or what really differs.
- **Update the docs** (English only; the matching page in `docs/pages/` – a new page = a new file + an entry in `docs/toc.json`; mark advanced topics
  with `data-level="advanced"` and passages that only add a column name, an internal, an exception or a difference to Forte with `data-level="detail"` (0.8.884); **no version numbers in the text** – history is CHANGELOG.md; page template and rules in the comment of toc.json), the **tutorial** (`docs/nunext-tutorial.htm`, 0.8.328 – lessons for the
  globeadmin, structure in its header comment; lesson ids are progress keys, never rename them) and **`TODO.md`** when relevant; remove TODO points that are done or
  outdated. **TODO.md is grouped by topic** (0.8.909, the owner: never by release): `## <topic>` (Decisions, SQL Server, Live server, Sign-in and security, Forms and design mode, Lists …) and
  one point per item `- [ ] **Short title** (0.8.x) – details`; a new "please verify" goes into its topic, not into a new section. The README stays short (what it is, requirements, install, updating, compatibility,
  licence) – new features are described in the CHANGELOG.md section of their version, in the docs and as one line in FEATURES.md (0.8.1012, the owner).
- **Upgrade baseline 0.8.321** (not released yet; both servers were on 0.8.321): no upgrade paths from earlier nuBuilder Next versions
  (old table names, old stored formats, old cookies, obsolete-file list – removed in 0.8.322). New migration steps and conversions are
  written for versions from the baseline on; the owner says when to move the baseline and drop the paths below it. nuBuilder
  compatibility (old nuBuilder databases, md5 passwords, missing nuBuilder tables) is NOT upgrade code and stays.
- **Do not add new variables to `nuconfig-sample.php`** unless asked.
- **Existing nuBuilder Forte databases are allowed** (0.8.1008, the owner; blocked 0.8.380–0.8.1007): the installer accepts a database with
  Forte's tables after the tick `backup_ack` (#insBackup: back up database + files, e.g. phpMyAdmin / Adminer / mysqldump) plus the
  integrity check; a hand-written nuconfig.php on a Forte database not set up yet shows #nuLoginBackup on the sign-in page
  (`NuInstall::backupNeeded()`) until the globeadmin's first sign-in adds the own tables. `db_origin` ('nunext' / 'legacy' / 'nubuilder')
  is still recorded; `$nuConfigAllowExistingNuDB` and the marker `_install/allow-existing-db` are no longer read. e2e `existing_db`,
  `gap_schema`, dev/tests/install.js (second part).
- **nuBuilder compatibility first**: same tables, same JSON formats (reports, SQL builder, form layouts), same PHP/JS function names
  and results as nuBuilder 4.9 – a form or report must keep working in nuBuilder. `dev/tests/compat.js` checks it against nuBuilder
  itself (0.8.364). Rules found that way: nuBuilder writes only EDITED fields (a new record's untouched empty field stays NULL –
  nuruntime.js `untouchedNew`; NuDB::update skips unchanged columns of nuBuilder's zzzzsys_ tables, NULL = ''); posted line ends are
  LF (index.php); child rows keep their ids (SQL builder clauses); JSON keeps the stored value types (report layout: `NuReport::keepTypes`,
  borderWidth / fontSize as text); negative numbers `€ -99,95` (minus after the prefix).
- **Main buttons on the left** (the owner, 0.8.1493): the mouse is usually on the left – put Next / Save / the main action of a bar at the
  left, next to Back, not pushed to the right edge (the setup wizard's bar: Back · Next · steps).
- Explanatory texts under fields are usually not wanted any more: show them as **tooltip of an (i) icon** next to the label
  (pattern below). The owner asks for this field by field.
- Requests are short; ask only when a wrong guess is expensive. When the plan has several parts the answer is often "ok all".
- **No effort or time estimates** in suggestions or answers (no "one to two weeks", "S / M / L", "a day of work") – the work is done by AI
  and goes much faster; describe scope and steps instead.
- On "suggest how …": give concrete proposals with numbered options (A/B/C … with 1/2/3) and a recommendation; implement the one picked
  ("do recommendations" = the recommended ones). Answers to the owner are short: what changed, where, what to verify.
- Every delivery: both zips are sent with the file tool, also when only docs changed.
- **German** (0.8.315): `lang/de.php` is German German (de-DE: ß – außerhalb, Größe – and „quotes“); `lang/de-CH.php` (Swiss: ss, «»)
  is GENERATED from it by `dev/lang_variants.php` – never edit it. Neutral wording, no "Sie".

## Architecture
```
index.php            router: ?p=<route> → pages/nu<route>.php ($routes list with json/public flags), CSRF check on every POST,
                     login guard, maintenance mode, demo-mode guard. nuBuilder auto login ?u=… → pages/nuautologin.php
api.php              REST API entry (api.php/v1/…) → core/nuapi.php
core/nuapp.php       NU_VERSION, NU_AUTOLOAD (feature classes loaded on first use – add new class-only files there), requires the
                     rest, config (nu_config / nu_cfg), security headers + CSP, strict sessions (cookie path = folder), helpers
core/nudb.php        NuDB: PDO wrapper for mysql, sqlsrv and sqlite (0.8.1521): run/all/one/value/insert/update/delete/page,
                     tableExists, ident(), quote(), session(); every query passes NuDemo::check(); NuDB::$statements / $nanos count what a
                     request asked (shown in developer mode, see "Performance"). NuMemo (same file, 0.8.1341): values of ONE request –
                     NuMemo::get(key, [tables], loader) / put / has; a write through NuDB that names one of the tables drops them ('*' = any
                     write), a rollback after a write forgets all, NuDB::pdo() switches it off (the raw connection is out of sight)
core/nusqlite.php    NuSqlite (0.8.1521): SQLite as a third database – setup() (WAL, busy timeout, PHP functions for MySQL's, NOCASE = case +
                     accent insensitive, LIKE with backslash escape), prepare() in NuDB::run / exec: MySQL's SQL translated at run time
                     (strings by MySQL's rules – "…" = string, backslash escapes; INSERT IGNORE, ON DUPLICATE KEY, INTERVAL, GROUP_CONCAT,
                     information_schema as sub-queries, SHOW / DESCRIBE, CREATE / ALTER TABLE incl. table rebuilds, DROP of views), fromDump()
                     for imports (NuSqlImport); own SQLite-only SQL quotes names with backticks (NuDB::quoteName) and escapes LIKE with '!',
                     never "name" or '\' (both would be read as MySQL strings). Not on SQLite: App cloner, phpMyAdmin, InnoDB conversion
core/nuauth.php      login, sessions (cookie nunext), password hashing (bcrypt, md5 upgrade; md5 storage while $nuUseMd5PasswordHash is on,
                     NuAuth::md5Storage), NuAuth::user(), loginToken() (one-time login links), demo globeadmin sessions (via 'demo_ga')
core/nuacl.php       rights from zzzzsys_access_form; constants FORM_* = nuBuilder's system form ids (nuuser, nuaccess, nuform …)
core/nuinfra.php     own tables (NuSchema::ensure/create; 0.8.462 gap analysis B2: ensure() changes the database only when mayChange() –
                     CLI, NuSchema::allow() (installer, the globeadmin's sign-in in NuAuth::start), or present() + a signed-in session and
                     not NuInstall::existingBlocked() (also true before the set-up on a database with nuBuilder's tables); NuAuth::startupHook
                     refuses other users with login_not_ready while !present(); e2e gap_schema), NuLog::write(event, object, recordId, details), tokens, sessions,
                     nu_client_ip() (last X-Forwarded-For entry), nu_https() (also behind a TLS proxy), nu_base_url()
core/nusecurity.php  NuSecurity (0.8.207): security settings (JSON row 'security'), login rate limit (counted in the log),
                     admin-equivalent rights (ADMIN_FORMS / restricted()), password confirmation (requireRecentAuth), protectDir,
                     secret masking (secretName, demoMask – also sus_json secrets), library versions, checks() for ?p=securitycheck,
                     mailBaseUrl(), reauthMinutes() (longer in developer mode: setting reauth_minutes_dev), setting login_token
core/nuhousekeeping.php  purges (tokens, sessions, API rows, log retention, temp files, API report PDFs) and the demo data reset –
                     nucron.php (CLI) or now and then a page view
core/nudemo.php      demo mode: users ($nuConfigDemoUser) and nuBuilder's demo globeadmin ($nuConfigIsDemo, gaDemo()), request / JSON / database
                     guards, HIDDEN_TABLES + touchesHidden() for typed SQL, save limit (SAVES_PER_HOUR 10 per IP, 100 overall)
core/nudemodata.php  demo data snapshot (backup folder, demo-snapshot.json) and reset of the allowed forms' tables (setting 'demo_reset')
core/nuaudit.php     audit trail (zzzzsys_nunext_audit, one row per changed column, change_id per save): begin()/record()/end() around
                     NuRecord::save/delete (sub-form rows too, form_id = the form the change was made in) and NuImport::run; settings row
                     'audit' {on, keep_days, exclude} (on defaults to $nuConfigLogRecordChanges, replaces the log's record_* events);
                     history(), restore() (value only while unchanged since, deleted row only while the key is free), purge() (housekeeping);
                     pages ?p=audit (NuListDefs 'audit', pseudo form nunextaudit, admin right 'audit') and ?p=audithistory&f=&r=
core/nunotify.php    notification centre (0.8.357): zzzzsys_nunext_notification (+ _user: recipient / read / hidden / confirmed per user), audience JSON
                     {all, users, departments, teams, positions, access}, mode live (matched when read) / fixed (stored at publishing, always for
                     must_confirm); senders: globeadmin, admin right 'notifications' = change, permission items NUNEXT_NOTIFY / NUNEXT_NOTIFY_OWN (own
                     department / team); the bell = nu_bell() in core/nulayout.php + assets/js/nunotify.js (polls ?p=notifyapi every 60 s; GET does not
                     refresh the idle time – NU_PASSIVE), pages/nunotifications.php (mine / manage / edit / receipts), nuNextNotify(), API /v1/notifications
core/nuselftest.php  self-test (?p=selftest, developer mode only): integration checks in groups system / data / forms / rights / features;
                     form checks write into the sample application and clean up (also audit rows); new checks = entries in checks()
core/nudbreset.php   Reset database (0.8.628, ?p=dbreset + `php nucron.php reset --sql=<file> [--check] [--backup] [--sample] --yes`, globeadmin
                     + MySQL / SQL Server; no developer mode needed since 0.8.1488): drops ALL tables / views, imports a nuBuilder Forte SQL file (NuInstall::checkDump($sql, true) = any table,
                     importSql), upgradeTo49, then as nu_install_finish (NuSchema, origin 'nunext', security defaults, NuMigrate); session re-touched
core/nudbstate.php   NuDbState (0.8.645): is the database ready? check() → ok / empty (no zzzzsys_form/_user/_access/_setup) / partial (a table of
                     the install scripts missing) / old (MySQL: a column of install/nubuilder49_tables.sql missing = Forte < 4.9); ready() cached in
                     the session (nu_db_ready = version|host|db). index.php: globeadmin → ?p=dbsetup (pages/nudbsetup.php, public route: globeadmin
                     or setup token / install.allow; empty → install scripts or Forte dump + upgradeTo49, partial → scripts again (IF NOT EXISTS /
                     INSERT IGNORE), old → explained only; setUp() then NuSettings / NuSchema / origin nunext / NuMigrate), others 503; NuAuth::login
                     refuses non-globeadmins (login_not_ready), NuAuth::start creates own tables only when ready; login hint #nuLoginDbSetup; e2e db_setup

```
Own tables: `zzzzsys_nunext_recent` (side bar, 0.8.460), `zzzzsys_nunext_log`, `_form_history`, `_user_2fa`, `_token` (kinds: pw_reset, report_file …), `_session`, `_setting` (key/value:
`security`, `backup_keep_sql`, `demo_reset`, `mail_auth_type`, `listview_defaults`, `audit`, `notify`, `home_fav_only_ga` …), `_api_*`, `_dashboard`,
`_widget`, `_admin_role`, `_admin_user`, `_list_view` (list views and saved start page layouts – with `fav` favourites), `_audit` (audit trail),
`_notification` + `_notification_user` (notification centre), `_form_draft` (form drafts), `_page` (page forms), `_migration`. Settings pattern (0.8.827 / 0.8.1341): `nu_setting_value($id)`, `nu_setting_has($id)`, `nu_setting_values([ids])` – ALL rows come with ONE
read per request (values over 8 KB – Global JS / CSS, the home hook cache – only when asked for; dropped by every write to the table through NuDB – `NuDB::noteWrite` – and after 2 s in long jobs)
and `nu_setting_save($id, $row[, $db])` (update or insert; a JSON setting: `nu_setting_json($id)` / `nu_setting_json_save($id, $data, $by)`, 0.8.1357) in core/nuapp.php, after `NuSchema::ensure()` where the table may be missing; never
write the SELECT / COUNT + update / insert by hand again. `NuDB::tableExists()` is cached per connection from ONE listing of all tables (`tableNames()`), refreshed by DDL through NuDB.

## Conventions
- **Entry files in the root** (0.8.825 / 0.8.826): `index.php`, `api.php`, `oauth.php` (addresses other systems call – REST clients, OAuth redirect URIs registered at Google / Azure: never renamed) plus `nucron.php`, `nudbadmin.php` – thin wrappers that load core/; new ones get the nu prefix.
- **Every PHP file of core/, core/form/, pages/, lang/ starts with** `<?php` / `declare(strict_types=1);` / `defined('NU_ROOT') || exit;`
  (direct web calls end at once; `dev/tests/run.php` checks it). New folders get the deny-all `.htaccess` (`NuSecurity::DENY`).
- **Dangerous globeadmin pages / actions** call `NuSecurity::requireRecentAuth(nu_url('<route>'))` (password again after 15 min).
- **Rights on nuform / nuprocedure / nuselect / nusetup / nuuser / nuaccess are admin-equivalent** – new code that grants rights or
  assigns access levels must respect `NuSecurity::restricted()` (see pages/nuaccesslevel.php, nuuser.php, core/nuapi.php).
- Ad-hoc SQL typed by users: `NuDemo::readOnlySql()` + `nu_db()->readOnly(fn)`; never show SQL error details to non-globeadmins.
- New log events with possibly secret values: `NuLog::diff()` masks names matching `NuSecurity::secretName()`.
- **Strict CSP** (`script-src 'self'; style-src 'self'`): no inline `<script>`, no `onclick=`, no `style=` attributes and no
  `<style>` injected by JS. Setting `element.style.x` from a JS file is fine. Page behaviour goes into `assets/js/<page>.js`; pass
  data via `data-*` attributes. Form pages relax the CSP for nuBuilder form code.
- Escape output with `h()`. Links with `nu_url('route', [params])` (null/'' params are dropped). Assets with `nu_asset()` (version
  stamp). POST forms need `nu_csrf_field()`. Read input with `nu_post()`, checkboxes with `nu_check()` ('1'/'0').
- **Both databases**: write SQL that works on MySQL and SQL Server, or branch on `nu_db()->type` ('mysql' / 'sqlsrv' / 'sqlite'; SQLite reads MySQL's
  SQL through NuSqlite – a branch is only needed for what NuSqlite cannot translate: `UPDATE … JOIN`, routines, user variables).
  **Joins / comparisons between nuBuilder Next's own tables and nuBuilder's tables** (e.g. `zzzzsys_nunext_log.user_id` = `zzzzsys_user.zzzzsys_user_id`)
  go through `nu_db()->sameText('a.col', 'b.col')` – live databases mix collations (utf8mb4_general_ci / utf8mb4_unicode_ci → MySQL error 1267, 0.8.331).
  Paging with `NuDB::page()`. New ids with `nu_id()` (nuBuilder style).
- Rights: `NuAcl::can(NuAcl::FORM_X, 'save'|'delete'|'clone'|'add')`, `NuAcl::require(...)`. Admin pages (formerly globeadmin only) use
  **`NuAdmin::require('<right>')`** (core/nuadmin.php, 0.8.323: view on GET, change on POST; `NuAdmin::can($right, 'view'|'change')`) – a new
  admin tool gets an entry in `NuAdmin::RIGHTS` (+ `NuHome::TILE_RIGHTS`). `['globeadmin']` checks are left for things only the real globeadmin does.
- Log every change: `NuLog::write('event_name', table, id, details)`; the log shows `t('ev_event_name')` – add that key.
- **Secrets in the database**: a new table with secrets is either `zzzzsys_nunext_*` or goes into `NuDemo::HIDDEN_TABLES`; secret keys in
  `sus_json` are masked by `NuSecurity::demoMask` (LOGIN_TOKEN, 2FA_, OTP_, PASSWORD_, NUNEXT_AUTOLOGIN) – extend the pattern for new ones.
- **Public links with a token** (one-time login, report PDF): random token, only its hash stored (`NuToken` or HMAC), limited time / use,
  used up in ONE conditional UPDATE (compare and swap), logged; the page is a `'public' => true` route.
- **Settings of nuBuilder Next itself** (not nuconfig variables): a row in `zzzzsys_nunext_setting` or a key of the Security settings
  (`NuSecurity::DEFAULTS` + the form in pages/nusecuritycheck.php); nuBuilder's tables get no new columns.
- Demo mode (`core/nudemo.php`): POSTs are refused except `READ_POSTS` (actions that only read) – add new read-only POST actions there.
- **Performance** (0.8.1341, the review of November 2026 – measured, keep it so): (1) what several places of one request read goes through
  `NuMemo::get()` – the user's row `NuAuth::userRow($id)`, the rights of a level `NuAcl::levelRows($access)`, hook code `NuFormMeta::phpById`,
  the setup row `NuMailer::setupRow()`; never a single-row SELECT of the same thing in a loop (lookups of sub-form rows: `NuLookup::prime`).
  Shared read helpers (0.8.1351, code review R5 / R6 / R9): access levels `NuAcl::levels()` (id => row, by code) / `level($id)` /
  `levelExists($id)` / `levelCodes()` / `levelsByGroup()`; forms `nu_forms()` (+ status, table, real) / `nu_form($id)` / `nu_form_exists($id)`;
  users `NuAuth::userRow($id)` / `userExists` / `userLogin` / `userLabel` – never write `SELECT … FROM zzzzsys_access` / a plain form or user
  read by hand again (record pages that edit a row keep their own fresh read). A cache of a database read is a NuMemo entry, not a
  `static $x` or a static property (`NuMemo::find($key, $v)` + `NuMemo::keep($key, $tables, $value)` for values built in steps,
  `NuMemo::drop($key)` after a change made another way); static caches stay only for files, config and per-request constants.
  (2) Settings only through `nu_setting_*` (one read). (3) A page view writes nothing it does not have to (recently used: not the page that
  is already on top; no DROP TABLE of the list's temp table when no code ran). (4) Work that needs no session gives up the session lock:
  `nu_session_release()` – never before code of the application runs (procedures, form code) – and `nu_session_write(fn)` to store
  something afterwards (dashboard widgets, report view, list / form exports). (5) CSS: **`:has()` only on the element a rule styles**
  (`.a:has(.b) { }`), never in front of it (`.a:has(.b) .c { }`, `body:has(…) …`, `tr:has(+ tr:hover) > *`): with such a rule Chromium works out
  the styles of every element the rule could reach again on EVERY change of the page (2–30 ms each; the help page 24 ms). Ask from the
  element itself (`.c:is(.b ~ *, :has(~ .b))`), or set a class on the server (`nu_body_class()`, `nuHasDash`, NuRender grid rows
  `nuRowTall` / `nuRowNoLabel`, body `nuSheetMain`) or from what the page printed (`nu_layout_buffer()`: `nuRecKeep`, `nuListBack`) –
  since 0.8.1342 there is NONE left, and run.php fails on any new one in assets/css or apps/*/assets. (6) JS: no custom property on `<html>` with the value it already has (each write restyles the whole page –
  nuapp.js `--nu-nav-h` / `--nu-sticky-*`), no `getComputedStyle` in a loop that also changes the page (nuruntime.js `cssRgb` keeps what it
  read). (7) `dev/tests/perf.js` compares the database statements of ~20 pages with `dev/tests/perf-budget.json` (developer mode: `#nuPerf`
  at the end of every page); e2e `perf_statements` has generous limits. A change that is meant to add statements: `perf.js --write`.
  (8) MySQL (0.8.1342): nuBuilder Next's own statements are sent in ONE step (PDO emulated prepares on the connection, −40 % per
  statement); code of the application runs with server-side prepares – `nu_db()->native(fn)` around NuPhp::run (procedures, form events),
  nuRunQuery, readOnly() (SQL typed by users) and statements with bound hash variables; `NuDB::pdo()` switches the rest of the request
  to server-side prepares (custom pages). Multi statements are off on the connection. Own SQL must not bind a value where MySQL wants
  a number (`LIMIT ?`) – write the number into the SQL (NuDB::page does). (9) HTML pages are gzip-compressed by `nu_compress_start()`
  (index.php; only a page written in one go, not JSON / downloads / flushed output); the form token is MASKED per call
  (`nu_csrf_token()` = random bytes + token XOR them, `nu_csrf_valid()` accepts masked and plain) – never compare a token by hand.
  (10) `dev/minify.py` (run by release.py) writes `.min.js` / `.min.css` next to every file of assets/ + `assets/min.json`;
  `nu_asset()` serves the copy outside developer mode (cookie `nunext_assets=src|min` overrides; e2e `ASSETS=min`). JS that finds its
  own address must accept the `.min` name (nucodetools.js). (11) The help is cached per reader group in temp/help/ (pages/nuhelp.php). (12) Form definitions (0.8.1343, P6 A): NuFormMeta keeps the forms
  a loaded form names as sub-form / lookup ($wanted) and loads them together on the first miss (prime(): one statement per table), the
  hooks BB … AD of every loaded live form in one statement (primePhp → NuMemo 'php:<id>'); drafts in a preview never go through prime();
  NuFormMeta::reset() for tests, liveStatus() reads sfo_status from the loaded row. Field access rules: one read of the whole table.
- **A failure the code goes on without** (0.8.1353, code review Q1): `catch (Throwable $ig) { nu_ignored($ig, 'why'); }` – one line in the
  server's error log while `$nuConfigDevMode` is set in nuconfig.php, nothing otherwise (reads only the config file). Only a failure that
  is EXPECTED in normal use may keep an empty block, with a comment containing "expected"; run.php fails on any other empty catch.
- **Sessions** (0.8.1353, Q5): read another session with `session_id($id)` + `session_start(['read_and_close' => true, 'use_cookies' => false])`
  – never change `use_only_cookies`, `use_trans_sid`, `sid_length`, `sid_bits_per_character`, `trans_sid_*`, `referer_check` (deprecated in
  PHP 8.4; run.php checks).
- **Session keys** (0.8.1354, code review R10): `$_SESSION[NuSess::X]` – every key is a constant of core/nusess.php with one line saying what
  it holds; a new key = a new constant there. run.php fails on a key written as text (core / pages / root; nudbadmin.php = Adminer's own).
- **SQL that differs per database** (0.8.1354, R8): NuDB `stamp(?ts)` (datetime parameter), `tableOptions()`, `castText()`, `jsonText(col, path)`,
  `firstRows(select, n)`, `limitTail(n)`, `addColumnSql()`, `dropTableSql()`, `quoteName()`, `savepointSql()` / `rollbackToSql()`, `label()` –
  use them instead of a new `$db->type === 'sqlsrv' ? … : …`; one-off catalogue queries keep their own branch.
- **System lists** (0.8.1354, L3): one file per list, `core/lists/nulist_<key>.php` = a trait with the list's method (it runs in NuListDefs'
  scope: `self::col()` …), used by NuListDefs and listed in NU_AUTOLOAD; core/nulistdefs.php keeps get(), the shared helpers and ADV.
- **Comments say why, not when** (0.8.1355 / 0.8.1358, Q6): no version numbers in comments – the history is CHANGELOG.md; only data or links
  of older versions keep theirs ("stored before 0.8.x", "until 0.8.x"; run.php checks PHP). Lines stay below 200 characters (L5): code
  is wrapped; templates break between the attributes of a tag; long strings and text may stay.
- **Pages do not do the work** (0.8.1354 / 0.8.1355, R7): a page's POST actions are `Nu<Route>Page::post(array $nuIn): array` in
  core/nu<route>page.php (the page's variables in, what the page shows afterwards out), the update page `NuUpdatePage`, the user form
  `NuUserPage` (core/nuuserpage.php; Save takes and returns the form state as arrays), the list / record preparation and the helpers of the
  form page `NuFormPage` (core/form/nuformpage.php). New actions go into these classes; a page never defines a global function.
- **The form runtime is joined** (0.8.1354, L1): edit `dev/src/nuruntime/<NNN-topic>.js` (pieces of ONE function – they share its variables),
  never assets/js/nu/nuruntime.js: `python3 -I dev/nuruntime_join.py` writes it (release.py runs it first; run.php fails when it differs).
  Sync the joined file to the test site after a join.
- **Style sheets** (0.8.1354 / 0.8.1355, L2): `python3 -I dev/css_group.py <file.css> [--write]` moves a section (a comment at the line start
  and its rules) to the last earlier section of the same component (COMPONENTS in the script) when nothing in between competes;
  `python3 -I dev/css_fold.py <file.css> [--write]` folds a later rule of the same selector and context into
  the first one when nothing in between sets a property of the same family (cascade unchanged); check pages with the computed-style
  comparison afterwards. A NEW correction goes into the rule it corrects, not into a block at the end of the file.
- **e2e tests** (0.8.1354, L6): dev/tests/e2e.js = helpers + runner; the tests are in `dev/tests/e2e/<area>.js` (read into e2e.js's scope –
  they use login, open, sql, expect … directly); the run order is `dev/tests/e2e/order.txt`, unlisted tests run after it, logout last.
  A new test goes at the end of the file of its area.
- Long tasks / downloads: `session_write_close()` first (or `nu_session_release()` when something is stored in the session later); `nu_time_limit(0)` where needed. Functions hosts may disable
  (disable_functions) are never called directly – `@` does not stop "Call to undefined function": use `nu_time_limit()`,
  `nu_ignore_abort()`, `nu_ini_get()`, `nu_ini_set()` or a `function_exists()` check (disk_*_space, posix_*, opcache_*) (0.8.273).
- **Third-party code** (0.8.409, `vendor/`): `vendor/bundled/` = libraries of the package (an update replaces them, never deletes –
  `NuUpdate::isLibrary`), paths ONLY through the constants of core/nuapp.php (`NU_VENDOR`, `NU_BS`, `NU_BI`, `NU_TOMSELECT`, `NU_CODEMIRROR`,
  `NU_QRCODE`, `NU_JQUERY`, `NU_JQUERY4`, `NU_JQMIGRATE3`, `NU_JQMIGRATE4`, `NU_JQCONFIRM`, `NU_FORMATTER`, `NU_APEXCHARTS`, `NU_FONTAWESOME`; JS files: the literal `vendor/bundled/…`);
  `vendor/packs/` = library packs (NuLibs, `NU_PACKS`, `NuUpdate::isProtected`); `dev/libsrc/<slot>/` = sources of the packs release.py
  builds into `_install/` of nunext.zip (PHPMailer). Old places (read until the upgrade baseline moves): `assets/vendor/`, `third_party/`,
  `lib/PHPMailer`. Sync to a test site with `rsync -a --exclude /nuconfig.php --exclude /vendor/packs --exclude /_libsrc --exclude _backup` (`vendor/nuconfig.php`
  must come along). Pack sources for *Build library packs*: `../libsrc/<slot>/` next to the project, or `_libsrc/<slot>/` inside it
  (0.8.412, hosts with open_basedir; deny-all, protected, never packaged) – NuLibs::libsrcBases().
- **Libraries**: every library in `vendor/bundled/` has an entry in `vendor/bundled/libraries.json` (name, version, dir, file, detect
  regex, licence, use key `upd_libuse_*`, url = the project on GitHub, 0.8.891). Changing a library = change files + registry; `dev/release.py` checks that the file shows the
  registered version and copies the list into the manifest (update page: card Libraries, "Libraries that change").
- **NuListDefs columns**: override a key of `self::col()` / `badgeCol()` with `array_replace(self::col(…), [...])` – `col() + [...]`
  keeps col()'s own `select` / `sort` / `text` and silently drops yours (0.8.553); `+` only for keys col() does not set (`html`, `main` …).
- Lists: `nu_list_footer($total, $page, $per, 'route', $params[, class, param, anchor])` = the pager used everywhere.
- **Selects of forms, tables, procedures, reports are ALWAYS Tom Selects** (the owner, 0.8.1079). **Long selects** (15+ options: forms, procedures, users, time zones …) are searchable (0.8.536): `<select class="nuListTomSelect">` +
  `nu_ts_head()` in the head and `nu_ts_foot()` before the page's own script (options may carry `data-sub` = second line); in the form
  builder `search: true` in the field spec. JS that changes options later calls `sel.tomselect.sync()`.

### UI patterns (keep them consistent)
- **(i) tooltip** next to a label (outside the `<label>`, so a click does not toggle a switch): **`nu_info_tip($text[, $class = 'ms-1', $wide = true, $trigger = '', $doc = ''])`** – `$doc` = id of the documentation section a long tap on touch screens opens (0.8.1374; give it whenever a matching section exists, else the page's F1 chapter is used)
  (core/nulayout.php, 0.8.1350) – never write the span by hand again (it is
  `<span class="nuInfoTip ms-1" tabindex="0" role="img" data-bs-toggle="tooltip" data-bs-custom-class="nuTipWide" aria-label="…" title="…"><i class="bi bi-info-circle"></i></span>`).
  Tooltips are initialised globally in `nuapp.js`.
- **Shared script helpers** (0.8.1350, assets/js/nuutil.js – loaded on every page after nubs.js, before nuapp.js and the page's script; NOT in
  `<head>` – nutheme.js cannot use it): `NuUtil.el(tag, 'class' | {class, text, html, on<event>: fn, attributes …}, text | node | [children])`,
  `NuUtil.esc(v)`, `NuUtil.csrf()` (nuNext.csrf, else `<meta name="nu-csrf">` of nu_head, data-csrf, _csrf input), `NuUtil.post(url, body, {csrf,
  strict, raw, keepalive, signal, headers})` (an object goes as JSON; answers the parsed JSON, {ok: false, error: 'HTTP n'} when not JSON unless
  strict), `NuUtil.toast(text, ok, {box, action: [label, fn], ms})`, `nuStore.get(k[, default]) / set / remove` (+ `nuStore.session.*`) instead
  of try { localStorage… } catch. New page scripts use them instead of a local el / esc / fetch with the token. PHP: **`nu_json($data[, $status])`**
  (core/nuapp.php) for every JSON answer (status, JSON in UTF-8, nosniff, no-store, exit).
- **Buttons of forms** (input button + run objects, `.btn.nuFormBtn`, 0.8.621 – nuBuilder Forte's solid look): accent colour
  (`--nu-accent`, text `--nu-on-accent` from NuBranding), hover / pressed through `filter` (works on backgrounds of the form's CSS),
  one line with … ; nuruntime.js `fitButtons()` (after nuOnLoad, new sub-form rows, theme switch; `nuNext.fitButtons`) sets
  `nuBtnInkLight` / `nuBtnInkDark` on backgrounds the form's CSS sets and the full label as tooltip when cut. Run buttons get no
  icon of their own (new tab / popup = `data-nu-run-hint` + title). HTML objects: Forte's typography (nuform.css: 13 px in `.nuAbs`,
  headings / p with the browser's margins, specificity of a plain tag). The former light look = Global code template `s_btn_light`.
- **Record buttons** (0.8.199, nuBuilder style): `btn nuBtnAct nuBtnSave` + `bi-floppy`, `btn nuBtnAct` + `bi-copy` (Clone),
  `btn nuBtnAct nuBtnDel` + `bi-trash`. Save turns red via class `nuSaveEdited` – set automatically for `.nuBtnSave` when its form
  changes (nuapp.js); on phones the three show only their icon. Save buttons carry `data-nu-save` (Ctrl+S).
  The Back button next to them: `btn nuBtnAct` + `bi-arrow-left` (0.8.211). Toolbar buttons have the normal Bootstrap size (no
  `btn-sm`) everywhere – lists, own pages and the form runtime (0.8.252); list search fields are 350 px wide.
- **Page header** (0.8.763, review E1): own pages that are not a NuListView list start with `<?= nu_page_header($title, ['icon' => 'bi-…',
  'sub' => …, 'tip' => …, 'buttons' => '<button … data-nu-save>…', 'back' => url|false, 'crumbs' => [[label, url], …]]) ?>` (core/nulayout.php) –
  breadcrumb (inside the settings centre: Home / Settings / section, Back → section), h1, subtitle, buttons row. Save of a settings page goes
  into `buttons` (form="<id>"), never below the content; one Save per page.
- **Record pages** (0.8.778 / 0.8.779): the title bar of one record gets class `nuRecHead` – the heading is hidden (the breadcrumb names the record), only the buttons show; a heading with a badge or a control stays visible, compact. The page calls **`nu_body_class('nuRecPage')` before `nu_layout_start()`** (0.8.1341) – the compact look of record pages hangs on `body.nuRecPage` (run.php: every page that prints `.nuRecHead` has the call).
- **Empty lists** (0.8.763, E3): a text `lv_empty_<list key>` + NuListDefs keys `new_url` / `empty_icon` give a list its empty state
  (NuListView::emptyState); search / filters that hide every row show "Show all".
- **Dangerous confirmations** (0.8.763, E4): `nuConfirmAsk(text, onOk, okLabel, 'btn btn-danger', {title, details, note, cancelFirst: true})`;
  form[data-confirm] with a btn-danger OK focuses Cancel by itself. The form runtime's record delete uses it (names the record + sub-form rows).
- **Back** on every own page (0.8.256): `<?= nu_back_button() ?>` (core/nulayout.php) – first button of the buttons row. Since 0.8.1017 nuapp.js points it to the page before (trail in sessionStorage); the server's target is only the fallback – `data-nu-back-fixed` keeps it. Title bars
  (0.8.268) always as on the user form: `<div class="nuTitleBar">` h1 · optional `.nuTitleSub` · LAST a `<div class="d-flex flex-wrap gap-2">`
  with Back and the other buttons (`btn nuBtnAct`, normal size) – never buttons as direct children next to the h1. Target: the breadcrumb parent from its `$parent` map, otherwise Home; record pages link to
  their list. New pages: add the button (and a map entry if they sit below another page).
- **Lists**: `table nu-table` – header with the card's background (no grey band), light uppercase labels, a line below (0.8.208).
  Secondary title bar buttons on phones (0.8.371): `NuListView::page(…, ['more' => [['icon', 'label', 'href', 'title', 'blank', 'id'], …]])`
  – buttons from 576 px on, below that a ⋮ menu right of Add with these entries and Print (users list: Import).
- **Phones, tablets, touch** (0.8.1344, usability review G1–G18): no text below 12 px (.75rem) in our CSS; fields 16 px on touch screens
  (pointer: coarse) – a new field needs nothing; tap targets ≥ 24 px on touch screens (generic rules – new small controls: check with
  the target-size rule of axe); a new page gets the phone header "‹ parent" by itself when it has a breadcrumb or a Back button (nuapp.js
  M1 block); a <th> without text gets a span.visually-hidden name; a row that scrolls sideways needs overflow-x auto only (nuapp.js fades
  and reveals); grid sub-forms are cards on phones (nunext.phone = 'table' opts out).
- **Grey badges / chosen buttons** (0.8.1299): grey count / state badges = `badge text-bg-secondary` (light grey by nuapp.css), a chosen `btn-outline-secondary` is light blue by itself – never set dark grey fills (#6c757d) again.
- **Segmented controls** (0.8.783): a choice of one = `.btn-group` with `.btn-check` radios + `btn-outline-secondary` labels (styled automatically), or links / buttons with `.active` in a `.btn-group.nuSeg`.
- **Tabs**: plain Bootstrap `.nav-tabs`; the underline look (0.8.312: no boxes, grey text, selected = blue text + 2 px blue line below; before: nuBuilder boxes 0.8.266) comes from `nuapp.css` (`--nu-tab-*` variables, `--nu-tab-surface` = background the tabs sit on; dark mode included).
- Confirmations: `<form data-confirm="text" data-confirm-ok="Delete" data-confirm-class="btn btn-danger">`. Combined with `data-nu-once`
  the OK of the dialog uses `requestSubmit()`, so the button still gets its spinner (update page → Install).
- Unsaved-changes guard: `<form data-nu-guard>`. Send-once forms: `<form data-nu-once>` + `data-nu-busy="…"` on the button
  (and check on the server, e.g. one-time token – see "Back up now" in `pages/nuupdate.php`).
- **Bootstrap JavaScript only through `nuBs`** (`assets/js/nubs.js`, 0.8.244): `nuBs.modal(el[, opts])`, `.tooltip`, `.tooltipOf`,
  `.dropdown`, `.tab`, `.toast`, `.collapse`, `.ready()` – never `bootstrap.X…` in our JS (ESLint global `nuBs`; a new JS file that uses it starts with `/* global nuBs */`, for DeepSource, 0.8.939). Pages that load
  `bootstrap.bundle.min.js` load `nubs.js` right after it. `window.bootstrap` stays for nuBuilder form code.
- Dark mode: `data-bs-theme` on `<html>`; use Bootstrap variables (`var(--bs-…)`) or own tokens with a `[data-bs-theme="dark"]` variant.
- Icons: Bootstrap Icons only (nuBuilder forms may still use Font Awesome).

## Test environment (rebuild it in a new session)
Nothing of the earlier test setup survives; this is how it was done (Linux container, PHP 8.3, MariaDB, Playwright + Chromium):
1. MariaDB may be missing in a new container: `apt-get install -y mariadb-server rsync`. `service mariadb start`; `mysql -e "CREATE DATABASE nutest; CREATE USER 'nu'@'localhost' IDENTIFIED BY 'nu'; GRANT ALL ON nutest.* TO 'nu'@'localhost';"`
   (for App cloner tests also `GRANT ALL ON nutest_cl.* …`).
2. Copy the package to a test folder (e.g. `<scratch>/site`), open it once → installer writes `nuconfig.php`; or write it by hand
   (`$nuConfigDBHost = "127.0.0.1"; $nuConfigDBName = "nutest"; $nuConfigDBUser = "nu"`, globeadmin `globeadmin` / `nu`).
3. nuBuilder itself (for TCPDF, PHPMailer, Font Awesome, nubuilder4.sql): `git clone --depth 1 https://github.com/nuBuilder/nuBuilder.git <scratch>/nub`.
   Simplest (0.8.207): put the test copy **inside** it – `<scratch>/nub/nunext` – then nuBuilder's libraries are found and the URL is
   `/nunext/…` like on the live server (cookie path!). Import `nub/nubuilder4.sql`, then Admin tools → *Sample data* installs the sample
   application (users sample_anna … sample_eva, random passwords: set known ones with `UPDATE zzzzsys_user SET sus_login_password = …`).
4. Web server (from the nuBuilder folder, larger uploads for update packages):
   `(cd <scratch>/nub && setsid nohup php -d display_errors=1 -d error_reporting=-1 -d upload_max_filesize=32M -d post_max_size=32M -S 127.0.0.1:8099 -t . > <scratch>/srv.log 2>&1 &)`
   – **with `display_errors=1`** (0.8.553): PHP warnings then land in the page as on many live servers, and smoke / e2e fail on them;
   without it they only go to srv.log (an "Undefined array key" in the procedures list went unnoticed this way).
   **Exception: `compat.js`** – nuBuilder (same server) prints its own deprecation notices into its JSON answers (4.9.2 git HEAD,
   `nuSqlString::$originalSQL`) and its pages time out: run compat with a server started without `display_errors` (0.8.559).
   → `http://127.0.0.1:8099/nunext/`. MariaDB and the PHP server **stop between turns** – restart both before testing
   (`service mariadb start; curl -s -o /dev/null http://127.0.0.1:8099/nunext/ || (…start the server…)`).
   Known passwords for the sample users (the tests used `Test1234!`):
   `php -r 'echo password_hash("Test1234!", PASSWORD_BCRYPT);'` → `UPDATE zzzzsys_user SET sus_login_password = '<hash>' WHERE sus_login_name LIKE 'sample_%'`.
5. nuBuilder itself for `compat.js`: `<scratch>/nub/nuconfig.php` = nuBuilder's `nuconfig-sample.php` with `$nuConfigDBName = "nutest"`,
   user / password `nu` → `http://127.0.0.1:8099/` (same PHP server). Its own JS error `nuToast is not defined` (4.9.2) is only noted.
   Browser tests: Node + Playwright (`NODE_PATH=$(npm root -g) node test.js`), Chromium is preinstalled (do not run
   `playwright install`). Log in at `index.php?p=login` with globeadmin / nu. `dev/tests/smoke.js` (BASE=…/nunext/) opens every page.
   Login rate limit: failed logins stay in `zzzzsys_nunext_log` for 15 minutes – delete `login_failed` rows between test runs.
   The installer asks for the token unless PHP's built-in server is used from the same computer.
9. **OWASP ZAP** (0.8.883, part of every gate when present): `curl -sL -o zap.tgz https://github.com/zaproxy/zaproxy/releases/download/v2.17.0/ZAP_2.17.0_Linux.tar.gz`
   (GitHub release downloads work here; ~240 MB), unpack to `../zap/` (so that `../zap/zap.sh` exists; Java 21 is installed). Run alone:
   `BASE=… DB=… ZAP_MINS=15 node dev/tests/zap.js` – public pages without a session, then the globeadmin's pages (spider with
   `handleParameters: ignore_value` → ~150 URLs, passive rules, active scan capped by ZAP_MINS; ~12 min). Known alerts with reasons:
   `dev/tests/zap-known.json` (id + url / param regex); unknown Medium / High fail. The scan's scope leaves out the routes of `EXCLUDE`
   in zap.js (config / update / reset / cloners / file manager / custom pages / procedures / Global code / mail / webhooks / session
   pages) – they change files, run code by design or end the session. The database is dumped and restored (like e2e). Reports in
   `<tmp>/nunext-zap/report/zap.html`, ZAP's own log in `<tmp>/nunext-zap/zap.log` (`ZAP_OUT`). `NUNEXT_TEST_ZAP=0` skips it in release.py.
8. Library pack Uppy for the e2e test `uppy_upload` (skipped without it, 0.8.459): copy nuBuilder's `third_party/uppy/` to `../libsrc/uppy/`,
   `php dev/libpack.php ../libpacks ../nunext-signing.key uppy`, then Update → Libraries → *Upload a pack* on the test site.
7. Static analysis: PHPStan **2.1.17** (the version `.github/workflows/ci.yml` pins – newer ones report false alarms, e.g. 2.3.1
   `closure.unusedUse` on variables that are used) from github.com/phpstan/phpstan/releases/download/2.1.17/phpstan.phar to `../phpstan.phar`; ESLint:
   `npm install --no-save --prefix .. eslint@9.39.5 globals@15.14.0 terser@5.36.0 lightningcss-cli@1.28.2` (ONE command – npm removes packages it is not given; outside the project, release.py
   finds them; terser + lightningcss = the minified copies of dev/minify.py, 0.8.1342).
6. After copying changed files wait ~3 s (OPcache) before reloading. Sync the whole package with
   `rsync -a --exclude /nuconfig.php --exclude /vendor/packs --exclude _backup ./ <site>/` (0.8.409: `/nuconfig.php` – the shim
   `vendor/nuconfig.php` must be copied).

10. **SQLite test site** (0.8.1521): a second copy inside the nuBuilder folder (e.g. `<scratch>/nub/nunextsq`) with `$nuConfigDBType = "sqlite";
   $nuConfigDBName = "<full path>.db";` (no host / user), set up with `php -r 'require "core/nuapp.php"; NuDbState::setUp("scripts");'` + Sample
   data (sales + ticket). e2e on it: `SQLITE=1 BASE=…/nunextsq/ DB=nutest SITE=<site> node dev/tests/e2e.js` – the tests' mysql / mysqldump
   calls go to `php dev/tests/sqlq.php <site> query|dump|restore` (a whole-database dump = an SQLite file via VACUUM INTO). Keep a copy of a good
   database file to put back (`sqlq.php <site> restore <copy>` – restore copies, so give it a copy). CI job `sqlite` runs `dev/tests/e2e/sqlite.txt`.

Test database discipline: before a test that changes data, `mysqldump -unu -pnu nutest <tables> > <scratch>/pre.sql`; afterwards
restore it and remove what the new version created, so the next release tests its database step again – e.g.
`DROP TABLE zzzzsys_nunext_audit, zzzzsys_nunext_notification, zzzzsys_nunext_notification_user; DELETE FROM zzzzsys_nunext_migration
WHERE zzzzsys_nunext_migration_id IN ('0011-audit', '0012-notifications'); DELETE FROM zzzzsys_permission_item WHERE pme_ident LIKE
'NUNEXT_NOTIFY%'` (state at 0.8.359: migration rows up to 0010 applied; audit and notification tables absent). The smoke test also
runs the migrations – clean up after it the same way.

Gotchas seen while testing:
- Playwright `click()` waits for the navigation it started – use `{noWaitAfter: true}` to look at a page before it leaves.
- In a form with `<input name="action">`, `form.action` is that input – use `form.getAttribute('action')`.
- Screenshots right after a theme switch or typing can catch CSS transitions (colours half way) – wait ~400 ms.
- The App cloner's Run asks with `window.confirm` (accept the dialog in tests).
- SQL Server could not be tested here (image registries blocked, no pdo_sqlsrv): MSSQL code was checked with simulated catalog data
  and the T-SQL parser `sqlglot` – see the short list in `TODO.md` (older details: `dev/TODO-archive.md`).
- Never `pkill -f <name>`: it also matches the shell running the command and ends it – kill by PID (`pgrep -f …`, then `kill`).
- The test database mixes collations on purpose (zzzzsys_user utf8mb4_general_ci, own tables unicode_ci) and zzzzsys_user is MyISAM
  (no transactions – a "dry run" must check first and write afterwards).
- Mail without a real server: a small Python SMTP server on 127.0.0.1:2525 (scratch script) – switch *Use SSL* off for it; or
  `$nuConfigMailLog` (mails written to a file instead, NuMailer::mailLogFile()).
- REST API tests: get a token through the API playground's JSON action `token` (client_ref = the internal client id), then call
  api.php with `Authorization: Bearer …`; give the client's service user the needed rights / scopes for the test and take them back.
- Never use `pkill -f` / `pgrep -f` with a pattern that the shell command itself contains; find the server with
  `ps -eo pid,args | grep "[p]hp .*8099"`.
- Shell JSON / SQL with quotes: pass SQL to `mysql` through stdin (`execSync('mysql …', {input: sql})` in Node), not inside "…".
- Tom Select fields: type into `.ts-control input` of the wrapper and press Enter; options are `.ts-dropdown .option`.
- Long tap on phones: dispatch synthetic `TouchEvent`s (`touchstart`, wait, `touchend`) in `page.evaluate` (context with `hasTouch`).
- Hover delays: move the mouse in small steps (`page.mouse.move`) and wait – tile tooltips need 800 ms of rest.
- The bell polls `?p=notifyapi` every 60 s; a GET there does not refresh the idle time (`NU_PASSIVE`, index.php / NuAuth::user).
- `dev/release.py` refuses a version that is not higher than NU_VERSION: if a release must be rebuilt before it was delivered, set
  NU_VERSION back and remove its CHANGELOG.md section and manifest `changes` entry first (then run with `--no-checks`, the manifest
  signature test fails until the release re-signs it).

## Where things stand (November 2026, 0.8.1524)
Last gate 0.8.1524 (smoke, e2e 652, compat 110, installer wizard, ZAP 0 unknown medium / high; pushed 0.8.1520–0.8.1524; SQLite added; first runs stopped on e2e form_access_dialog – it compared level ids with the list sorted by code, a test application's level has id taccess / code TSALES – and forte_db2_compat – a customer code with a trailing space left by an earlier test; both tests fixed). Gate before: 0.8.1519 (smoke, e2e 652, compat 84, installer wizard incl. nuBuilder Forte afterwards, ZAP 0 unknown; pushed 0.8.1496–0.8.1519). Gate before: 0.8.1495 (pushed 0.8.1452–0.8.1495; published as v0.8.1495). Gate before: 0.8.1451 (smoke, e2e 610, compat 84, installer wizard incl. OLD_DUMP, ZAP 0 unknown medium / high; pushed 0.8.1438–0.8.1451; published as v0.8.1451; passed at the first run). Gate before: 0.8.1437 (smoke, e2e 600, compat 84, installer wizard incl. OLD_DUMP, ZAP 0 unknown; pushed 0.8.1421–0.8.1437; published as v0.8.1437; the first run stopped at once because MariaDB and the PHP server had stopped between turns – start both before a gate). Gate before: 0.8.1420 (smoke, e2e 589, compat 84, installer wizard incl. OLD_DUMP = Forte 4.5.1's nubuilder4.sql, ZAP 0 unknown; pushed 0.8.1414–0.8.1420; the first runs stopped on test problems: install.js OLD_DUMP gave mysql two database names (the gate's DB names nutest – fixed with USE), and design_list_width_zero failed because the gate's update tests install ../nunext.zip = the previous package without the fix – LESSON again: every code change needs a fast release BEFORE the gate). Never start a single e2e / compat test while a gate runs (both dump and restore the whole database).

- 0.8.1341 performance review (the owner: thorough analysis + build it in; usability and code review as lists in the Claude Doc "nuBuilder Next review – performance, usability, code"). Measured with scratch tools (general log per request, excimer, Chromium traces; an unchanged 0.8.1340 copy next to the test site, requests alternating): 25 pages 1,535 → 884 statements, time to first byte −29 % on a local database, style work in the browser −52 %. SERVER: settings in one read (nu_setting_rows; 683 of 2,598 statements were single settings reads); NuMemo (user row, level rights, permissions, home form, hook code, setup row, auto tile page, report rights, list filter options); NuNotify::me() from the user's own row (was: every user loaded on each page) + counts() for the bell; NuLookup::prime (sub-form lookups in one statement per column); NuReady::homeCard from the cached summary, NuTutorial::ids cached in the session, NuLanguages::variants memo, NuSample::versionInfo; NuSidebar::record skips the page already on top (SAME_SECONDS 300); NuBrowse::dropTempTable only when code ran (NuPhp::$runs); NuRowDetails::candidates cached in the session; session lock given up (nu_session_release / nu_session_write) for dashboard widget queries (4 widgets of 0.5 s: 2.0 s → 1.0 s, another page meanwhile 461 → 14 ms), report view / Excel (not procedure sources, not save / e-mail), form and list exports. BROWSER: bell asks nothing right after a page load unless the count names an unannounced id (#nuBell data-ids / data-confirm); nuruntime.js cssRgb reads rgb() directly and keeps other notations per theme (60 forced restyles on a list with two badge columns → 8); --nu-nav-h / --nu-sticky-top / --nu-sticky-h written only when they differ (CSS defaults for the sticky ones); :has() rewritten where it stood in front of the styled element (see Conventions → Performance): row highlight (border-top: 1px double transparent on the hovered row instead of tr:has(+ tr:hover) > * – pixel-identical), HTML objects with a dashboard (.nuHasDash), rows without columns in HTML objects (asked from the child), segmented controls (asked from the button), breadcrumb slot (body.nuNavCrumbsOn), record pages (body.nuRecPage via nu_body_class in 18 pages) – one change of the page cost 2–30 ms of style work, now 0.2–2.5 ms. Checked with full-page screenshots of ~110 pages old against new at 1440 / 820 / 390 px, dark, and as a user: identical except environment (the copy's folder) and a line that flickers in both. - 0.8.1290 Reset database in steps (the owner: 503 of the host after a while with a ~470 MB dump; plus a progress window as on the update page): pages/nudbreset.php action run (SQL file) → NuDbReset::startJob (job file _update/.dbreset-job.json with sha256 of a random token, check's fsize / mtime must match – dbr_err_changed; one job at a time) → page with modal #dbrProgress[data-nu-dbr-job / -step / -done] (#dbrBar, #dbrPhase) + assets/js/nudbreset.js (POST ?p=dbresetstep job=<token> in a loop, busy → 3 s, failures retried up to 60, then reload); ?p=dbresetstep is served in index.php BEFORE nu_boot (no session / sign-in: the token is the right; flock .lock → busy) → NuDbReset::step(): phases backup · unpack (NuSqlImport::plainCopy → _update/.dbreset-work-*.sql, NuSqlDump::spaceProblem) · drop · import (NuSqlImport::slice: from byte off until JOB_SECONDS 12 (≤ max_execution_time / 3), stops at a line end with no open statement, replays the dump's SET statements (time zone, sql_mode …) in the next slice, COMMIT + UNLOCK TABLES at the end) · finish (finishImport = run()'s end, NuLog with the job's user) · done / error; NuDbReset::afterJob() in index.php before NuAuth::user() keeps the user signed in (sessions table new); package resets and nucron.php reset unchanged (one go); nu_ignore_abort in run() / runPackage(); texts dbr_ph_* / dbr_job_* / dbr_err_job_* / dbr_err_changed / dbr_err_no_job / dbr_err_unpack / dbr_progress_*; docs 08-16; e2e db_reset (steps, work copy removed, modal)
